
> Read CLAUDE.md, then docs/decisions.md (esp. §3–6a, §8, §10–12, §14),
  content/page.md, content/email.md, content/calc-examples.mjs, data/sources.md
  §7–8 and data/NOTES.md. Phases 0–2 are approved. Start Phase 3 (Build).

  Scope — build locally, no deploy and no accounts yet:
  1. Astro (static output) + one small vanilla-TS island for the tool, per
  decisions §11. Section order and all copy
     from content/page.md exactly; every {…} filled from data/ at build time,
  never typed by hand.
  2. The tool: Step A (chips → twins, VTI/SCHD/VT special cases, "KID v
  češtině" label only where kid_cs is true),
     Step B (fee calculator: monthly amount, years, return 0–10 % default 5 %,
  broker choice incl. "Nevím"),
     Step C (dividend filter only; the currency filter was dropped, §14 #8).
  Ad-variant entry: ad_b_* opens on Step B
     with VOO preselected, anything else on Step A (§4).
  3. Fee calculator maths with TDD: it must reproduce node
  content/calc-examples.mjs for the default inputs
     (KB/ČSOB ranges, ETF 22 000 Kč) and produce the year 1/5/10/15/20 rows for
  the e-mail.
  4. EUR/CZK: build-time fetch from the ČNB API into data/fx.json with fallback
  to the last file (§14 #10). Propose
     the daily rebuild mechanism (Vercel cron + deploy hook vs GitHub Actions
  schedule) — don't set it up yet.
  5. Form + /api/lead as a Vercel function following §6a exactly (allow-list,
  server-side e-mail validation,
     honeypot, 24 h dedupe, existing contact = success). Brevo calls behind env
  vars with .env.example; TDD for
     validation and the allow-list. Rate limiting: propose the option, don't
  create accounts.
  6. Thank-you state, /plan page (textContent only), privacy page with
  [DOPLNIT] placeholders, footer disclaimers,
     no-JS fallback (static twin table + form works as a plain POST).
  7. Design per decisions §10 using frontend-design: ticker pair with
  strike-through, Archivo self-hosted and
     subset, semantic tokens, one user-triggered animation with
  prefers-reduced-motion.

  Process (log each skill and every subagent prompt verbatim in
  docs/ai-worklog.md):
  - superpowers:writing-plans first → ✋ show me the plan before writing code.
  - Then superpowers:subagent-driven-development for the build, so the README
  has real agent prompts to show.
    Review every subagent's output yourself before committing it.
  - TDD only for the calculator, form validation and /api/lead (CLAUDE.md
  overrides). Ponytail on full, but it must
    not remove a11y attributes, consent text, honeypot, server validation or
  source/date labels.
  - Tracking is Phase 4: don't add analytics yet.

  Rules:
  - Budget: JS < 60 kB gz on first load, LCP < 2.0 s on throttled 4G, no font
  CLS. Check the first screen of both
    heroes at 390×844 in a browser and screenshot it; if hero B doesn't fit,
  shorten its sub-line first.
  - GDPR legal basis for sending the plan and the optional newsletter checkbox
  is still VERIFY (§5): research it
    (GDPR art. 6/7, zákon 480/2004 Sb.) with sources before writing the privacy
  page; tell me if it changes the form.
  - Ask before: creating any account (Vercel, Brevo, Umami, Upstash),
  deploying, or adding a dependency beyond Astro.
  - Commit small with descriptive messages. ✋ Stop when the site runs locally
  with all tests green and show me:
    test output, bundle size, the two first-screen screenshots, and what's left
  for Phase 4–5.

● The Phase 3 plan is ready for your review:
  docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-phase3-build.md. It's committed as e5b81c4
  and has a worklog entry. No code is written yet.

  Approach
  - The logic lives in small TypeScript modules: calculator, input allow-list,
    plan model and e-mail renderer.
  - The tool, /plan and /api/lead all share those modules.
  - Tests run with Node's built-in node --test. Node 26 runs .ts files directly,
    so the only dependency is astro@7.3.8.
  - The calculator test has to reproduce the output of calc-examples.mjs: 22 000
    Kč for the ETF, 272 000–339 000 Kč for the bank funds, plus the year-20
    row.

  13 tasks in order:
  1. Scaffold the site.
  2. GDPR research (I do this one myself).
  3. EUR/CZK fetch from ČNB at build time.
  4. Broker data fields.
  5. Calculator (TDD).
  6. Allow-list and e-mail validation (TDD).
  7. Plan model and e-mail renderer (TDD).
  8. /api/lead (TDD).
  9. Static page, legal pages and the no-JS version.
  10. The tool.
  11. Forms, sticky bar and thank-you state.
  12. /plan.
  13. QA: tests, bundle size, screenshots.

  Subagents do tasks 1, 3 and 5–12. I review each one before committing.

  Decisions I need you to confirm or veto (all 17 are in the plan's table):
  - ČS fee: I count 0,35 % with the 4 EUR minimum on every purchase. The price
    list also has a regular order through internet banking with no minimum,
    which would make ČS much cheaper at 3 000 Kč a month. I chose the
    conservative option.
  - Which ETF fee the calculator uses: the highest TER among the twins shown.
    For VOO that's VUAA at 0,07 %, which matches calc-examples and doesn't
    flatter the ETF.
  - Twin order everywhere: accumulating first, then alphabetical by Xetra ticker
    (SPYL · SXR8 · VUAA). This follows page.md §9. data/NOTES.md says "same
    issuer first", so the two conflict and I picked page.md.
  - Slider ranges: 500–30 000 Kč a month, 1–40 years, 0–10 % return in 0,5 %
    steps.
  - Hero A or B: a tiny inline script in the page head picks the variant before
    the page paints, so there's no flash or layout shift. Without JS, visitors
    get variant A.
  - Local testing without Brevo: with no API key, /api/lead runs in dry-run and
    writes the e-mail to .dev/last-email.html. That file is also what I'll
    screenshot for the real e-mail preview in "Co vám přijde e-mailem".

  What you asked me to propose:
  - Daily FX rebuild: a GitHub Actions schedule that calls a Vercel deploy hook.
    A Vercel cron would need an extra function just to call the same hook. One
    catch: GitHub pauses scheduled jobs after 60 days with no repo activity.
  - Rate limiting: first choice is a Vercel firewall rate-limit rule on
    /api/lead, but I haven't verified that the free Hobby plan allows it. The
    fallback is a free Upstash Redis counter, which needs a new account.

  Checks before planning:
  - There's no headless Chrome or Edge on this machine, so the 390×844
    screenshots will use the built-in-browser skill. If it can't set that
    viewport, I'll ask before adding Playwright.
  - Astro's built-in font handling may not include Archivo's width axis, which
    the wide ticker style needs. The plan has a manual self-hosting fallback for
    that case.

  Does the plan capture what you want? I'll start Task 1 with subagents once you
  approve.

  Not checked yet: whether Vercel can resolve the .ts imports in api/ (needs a
  deploy, Phase 5). The GDPR research in Task 2 could still change the form; if
  it does, I'll stop and ask before building it.

> approved

  ⎿  Done (7 tool uses · 87.9k tokens · 53s)

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ✅ Spec compliant for the files and steps in the brief. Astro 7.3.8 is the
  only dependency. Scripts match verbatim. `Base.astro` has the required props,
  slot and inline script. All six tokens plus `--font` are defined, and
  `.env.example` matches the brief. `.dev/` is in `.gitignore`. The manual-font
  fallback was justified and reported. One brief-mandated defect breaks the
  no-JS requirement (Important #1).
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff:
    - The `dev`, `build` (via `prebuild`), `test` and `size` scripts reference
  `scripts/dev.mjs`, `scripts/fetch-fx.mjs` and `scripts/size.mjs`. The diff
  adds none of them, and the report only claims `npx astro build`. `npm run
  build` (what Vercel runs) will fail until a later task adds `fetch-fx.mjs`.
  The controller should confirm that task exists.
    - Whether `tnum` changes digit widths. The report defers this to Task 13.
    - The brief's `<Font cssVariable="--font" preload />` was dropped in favour
  of the manual font path. This is acceptable given the report's finding, but I
  did not re-check the claim that Astro's fonts config emits no `wdth` axis.

  ### Strengths
  - The implementer tried Astro's built-in fonts path first, found the missing
  `wdth` axis, and reported both paths with exact byte sizes and the perf
  concern.
  - Both subsets use Google's `unicode-range` split, with `font-display: swap`
  and preloads that carry `crossorigin`.
  - The inline script is in the `<head>` and runs before paint, so the hero
  variant does not flash.
  - Scope is clean: one dependency, no analytics code, no stray files, and
  `noindex` is handled via a prop.

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  1. **Both hero variants show without JS** (`src/styles/global.css` line 3967
  of the diff, with `src/layouts/Base.astro` line 3873). `data-variant` is set
  only by the inline script. Without JS, neither `[data-variant="a"] .only-b`
  nor `[data-variant="b"] .only-a` matches, so both heroes render. Decision §15
  #14 says no-JS means variant A. The CSS is as the brief specified; the brief
  missed the default. Fix: write `<html lang="cs" data-variant="a">` in the
  markup and let the script override it.
  2. **First-load font weight is about 176 kB, all preloaded**
  (`src/layouts/Base.astro` lines 3883-3884 of the diff). Both variable
  `wdth`+`wght` files are fetched at high priority on every page. Czech text
  needs both subsets, so neither can be dropped. On throttled 4G that is roughly
  0.9 s of bandwidth competing with the 2.0 s LCP budget. The report flags this
  but does not measure it. Check LCP in Phase 5. If it is tight, subset the
  `wdth` range to what is used (100 and 125), or serve a static-width 400/700
  pair for body text.
  3. **The fallback face will not stop layout shift on the target devices**
  (`src/styles/global.css` lines 3935-3943 of the diff). `local("Arial")` does
  not exist on stock Android, which runs Roboto and Liberation. There the
  `size-adjust` and override metrics never apply. The fallback also has no
  `font-stretch`, so the 125%-wide `.ticker` text shifts on swap on every
  device. This conflicts with the "no font CLS" constraint for a mobile-ad
  audience. Add `local("Roboto")` and `local("Helvetica Neue")` alternatives, or
  measure CLS in Phase 5 and tune.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)
  - `.gitignore` (diff context, last original line) reads `*.pdf.superpowers/`
  as one line. The `*.pdf` and `.superpowers/` rules are both broken, and `git
  status` confirms the PDFs show as untracked. This is pre-existing and not
  caused by this task, but the commit appended `.dev/` after it. Put each
  pattern on its own line.
  - `src/layouts/Base.astro` lines 3879-3880 hardcode `og:title` and
  `og:description`, so thank-you and legal pages inherit the landing page's
  text. Derive them from the `title` and `description` props.
  - `package-lock.json` root shows `"name": "etf_landing_page"` and `"astro":
  "^7.3.8"`, while `package.json` says `etf-dvojce` and `7.3.8`. The lock is
  stale. Regenerate it with `npm install`.
  - Fonts in `/public/fonts/` have unhashed names, so Vercel's default cache
  headers apply. Add long-cache headers in `vercel.json` when deploying.

  ### Assessment

  **Task quality:** Needs fixes

  **Reasoning:** The scaffold is clean and follows the brief, and the font
  deviation is well justified. Important #1 is a real no-JS regression (two
  heroes shown at once) that is a one-attribute fix. Important #2 and #3 put the
  font budget and CLS requirements at risk and need measuring or tuning before
  Phase 5. The missing `scripts/*.mjs` files will break `npm run build` until
  they land.

  **Finding Verdicts**

  1. **No-JS shows both heroes** — ADDRESSED. `src/layouts/Base.astro:~21` now
  has `<html lang="cs" data-variant="a">`. The inline script still overrides it,
  and the report says `dist` was checked.
  2. **Font split** — ADDRESSED. `src/styles/global.css` now has wght-only
  Archivo latin and latin-ext faces (34.9 kB and 32.6 kB) and a separate
  "Archivo Wide" face from `archivo-wide-800.woff2` (4.4 kB). Together that is
  about 72 kB, under the 100 kB target. The wide face declares weight 800 and
  `unicode-range: U+30-39, U+41-5A`. `.ticker` uses `"Archivo Wide",
  var(--font)` with no `font-stretch`. All three files are preloaded in
  `Base.astro`. The wdth variable files are gone (the diff shows both
  overwritten with the smaller files). The report says Google's CSS returned
  `font-weight: 800` and `font-stretch: 125%`, and the file sizes are consistent
  with that. I did not re-fetch it.
  3. **Fallback face on Android** — ADDRESSED. The src list is `local("Arial"),
  local("Roboto"), local("Helvetica Neue")` (`global.css`), with the `shortcut:`
  comment next to it.
  4. **.gitignore** — ADDRESSED. `*.pdf`, `.superpowers/` and `.dev/` are now on
  separate lines. The report says the root PDFs no longer show in `git status`.
  5. **og props** — ADDRESSED. `Base.astro` accepts `ogTitle` and
  `ogDescription`, defaulting to `title` and `description`.
  `src/pages/index.astro` passes the original og texts.
  6. **Stale package-lock.json** — ADDRESSED. The lockfile name is now
  `etf-dvojce` and `astro` is pinned to `7.3.8` with no caret.

  **New Breakage in the Fix Diff**

  - **Minor, `global.css` / `index.astro`:** the "Archivo Wide" `unicode-range`
  covers A–Z and 0–9 only. A `.ticker` element containing lowercase or
  diacritics, such as the stub `<h1 class="ticker">ETF Dvojče</h1>`, renders
  those glyphs in regular-width Archivo 800. A real ticker like "VOO" is
  unaffected. Later tasks should keep `.ticker` to uppercase tickers, or the
  wordmark will mix widths.
  - **Minor, `.ticker`:** if the wide font fails to load, the fallback is
  `var(--font)` at normal width. That is acceptable.
  - **Minor, report:** the fix report gives results but not the raw command
  output, such as the `git status` and `grep` of `dist`. The claims match the
  diff.

  **Out-of-Scope Observations**

  None.

  **Verdict**

  **Fix round:** All findings addressed, no new Critical/Important breakage.

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ✅ Spec compliant. All brief items are present:
    - URL, 8 s `AbortSignal.timeout` and EUR pick
  (`scripts/fetch-fx.mjs:36-41`).
    - `rate / amount` and the 15–40 sanity range (`:42-43`).
    - Keep-existing-file fallback with `console.warn` and `{kept:true}`, or a
  throw when no file exists (`:47-51`).
    - CLI entry using the Europe/Prague date (`:54-58`).
    - All 4 brief tests are verbatim (`scripts/fetch-fx.test.mjs`).
    - `data/fx.json` has the interface shape. The rate of 24.355 for 2026-10-14
  matches the brief's sources.md §7 reference.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from diff: how `npm test` picks up `scripts/*.test.mjs`
  (package.json is unchanged in this diff). The controller should confirm the
  glob includes `scripts/`. The report says the full `npm test` was run.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from diff: how and when the script is wired into the build
  (`package.json` prebuild) or the daily rebuild. This is outside Task 3's file
  list.

  ### Strengths

  - The code is minimal and has no extra dependencies. It uses only `node:`
  builtins and the global fetch.
  - `fetch` is injectable, so the tests need no network.
  - The failure paths are covered: network throw, HTTP 500, implausible rate,
  and no file.
  - `{ kept: false, ...fx }` returns the data without a second read.
  - The `import.meta.url` guard keeps the module importable by the tests without
  side effects.

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  None.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)

  - `scripts/fetch-fx.mjs:36` and `data/fx.json:4`: `source_url` carries the
  request date (`date=2026-10-15`), but ČNB returns the rate valid for
  2026-10-14. The implementer flagged this themselves. It is still a correct,
  reproducible URL, since ČNB resolves `date` to the latest published rate.
  Optionally add a README or `data/NOTES.md` line saying "validFor is
  authoritative".
  - `scripts/fetch-fx.mjs:43`: `rate >= 15 && rate <= 40` is a hand-typed range.
  It is a sanity bound, not data, so it is consistent with the brief. A
  `shortcut:` or why-comment is optional.
  - `scripts/fetch-fx.test.mjs:98-103`: the implausible-rate test only asserts
  `kept === true`, so it does not distinguish a 0 rate rejected by the range
  check from any other failure. Also, `validFor: 'x'` in the test input is
  unused. This is brief-verbatim, so it is not a defect of the implementation.
  - `scripts/fetch-fx.mjs:45`: the write is not atomic. A crash mid-write could
  corrupt the fallback file. This is negligible for a build-time script of this
  size.

  ### Assessment

  **Task quality:** Approved

  **Reasoning:** The implementation matches the brief point by point with
  minimal, correct code, and the tests cover the success path and each fallback
  path. The only notes are minor polish items on `source_url` date semantics and
  test strictness.

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ✅ Spec compliant. `src/lib/fees.ts` matches the brief's implementation line
  for line. The only addition is `export` on `Bank`, which the test imports.
  All 8 brief tests are present, with the oracle output pasted as a header
  comment (`src/lib/fees.test.ts:1-7`). Only the two new files changed, no
  dependencies were added, and there is no analytics code.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff: the commit trailer. The dispatch requires
  `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5`. The report says Haiku 5.5 was used, and
  this session's attribution reminder says Sonnet 5.5. The controller should
  check `git log -1 --format=%B 55bad02` and amend if Opus is wanted.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff: the oracle numbers and the "8 pass" run. I
  did not re-run the suite, per the instructions.

  ### Strengths

  - The data shapes match the types. I checked `data/brokers.json` and
  `bank-funds.json` against the `Commission`, `Tier`, `Broker` and `Bank` types:
    - XTB and T212 have `pct: 0` and `min_eur: 0`.
    - Fio has tiers with `fee_eur`, and a final tier with `fee_pct: 0.15` and
  `min_eur: 6.95`.
    - ČS has `pct: 0.35`, `min_eur: 4` and `custody_pct: 0.08`.
    - The bank funds with `ongoing_pct` null are filtered out, and `exit_pct`
  null falls back to 0.
  - The `pctOnly` guard on `fixedFeePct` (`fees.ts:149-151`) correctly keeps a
  pure 0,35 % fee from being flagged as a "fixed fee". A minimum-driven fee like
  ČS at 123 EUR (3,25 %) is flagged.
  - The sell-side commission is computed on the gross value (`fees.ts:134`). So
  Fio drops to the 0,15 % tier at exit, and a fixed fee is not wrongly charged
  as a percentage of a large pot.
  - The 0 % return test (`fees.test.ts:89-91`) pins the model to exact
  arithmetic.
  - Rounding happens only in `roundK`.

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  None.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)
  - `fees.ts:146`: if `banks` is empty or has no `ongoing_pct` values,
  `Math.min()` and `Math.max()` return `Infinity` and `-Infinity`, and those
  values flow into the UI. This is a build-time data problem, not a user input.
  A one-line assert would fail loudly at build instead.
  - `fees.ts:115`: `brokers.find(...)!` returns undefined on a bad id. A bad `b`
  URL param would then throw a `TypeError` in the island. Task 8 must validate
  `b` against `BrokerId` before calling this. This is out of scope for this
  diff, so please check it when that task is reviewed.
  - `fees.test.ts:79`: the test title says "the 20-year row equals compare()",
  but it asserts against hard-coded oracle numbers, not `compare()`. Either
  rename it or compare `rows[4]` to `compare(base, ctx)`. Coverage is otherwise
  adequate.
  - `fees.test.ts:73-76`: nothing tests the `fixedFeePct` threshold boundary or
  the ČS case. ČS is flagged at 3 000 Kč (3,25 %), but no test covers it. Its
  pure-percentage case, where `pctOnly` matters, is covered only indirectly
  through XTB. A single ČS assertion would lock the `pctOnly` branch.

  ### Assessment

  **Task quality:** Approved

  **Reasoning:** The implementation follows the brief exactly. Its fee model is
  consistent with the real broker and bank data, and the tests exercise real
  behavior against the oracle. The remaining items are Minor hardening and a
  trailer check for the controller.

● Agent(Review Task 6 (spec + quality)) Sonnet 5.5
  ⎿  Done (5 tool uses · 63.5k tokens · 29s)
  (ctrl+o to expand)

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ✅ Spec compliant. All six interfaces match the brief's signatures.
  `RANGES`, the `t, m, y, r, b, d` order (Ruling A, pinned by the extra test at
  selection.test.ts:48-52), the regex, the 254 cap, the control-character
  rejection and the `ad_a_*` / `ad_b_*` / `unknown` mapping are all present. The
  9 brief tests are present verbatim, plus one extra.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff: commit trailer. The report says it used
  `Co-Authored-By: Claude Haiku 5.5`, while the dispatch file requires `Claude
  Opus 5.5`. The diff has no commit message, so the controller should check `git
  log -1 --format=%B f038d44` and amend if Opus is wanted.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff: decisions §6a rule 1 and §7 are not in the
  diff. If §6a sets a length cap on the `t` parameter, it is not enforced (see
  Minor 3).

  ### Strengths
  - selection.ts has one job and no data import, since `knownTickers` is a
  parameter. Ticker filtering is by allow-list, so the output is bounded however
  large the input is.
  - Handling of empty or whitespace-only numerics (selection.ts:104-105) avoids
  the `Number('')` clamp-to-min trap.
  - `validateEmail` treats null and undefined as `empty` (:139). Its
  control-character check covers CR/LF header injection.
  - Tests exercise real behaviour with no mocks. The two extra assertions pin
  the `toParams` key order and the empty-`t` output.

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  None.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)
  1. selection.ts:157: the `adVariant` regex `(?:_|$)` accepts a bare `ad_a` and
  `ad_b`. §7 specifies `ad_a_*` only, and the report admits this is untested.
  Fix: use `/^ad_([ab])_/`, or add a test that makes the bare form deliberate.
  2. selection.ts:146: the order is strip, slice, then trim. The brief says
  trim, strip, slice. Leading whitespace now eats the 100-character cap, so a
  value with 100 or more leading spaces becomes `''` instead of keeping its
  text. The report discloses the change, but no test covers it. Fix:
  `.replace(...).trim().slice(0, 100)`. Optionally also strip `\u0085`, `
  ` and `
  `.
  3. selection.ts:112: the `t` string is split with no length cap before the
  allow-list filter. The result is bounded, but `split` still runs on whatever
  the URL carries. This only matters if §6a requires a cap on `t`.
  4. selection.ts:136: `EMAIL_RE` is verbatim from the brief. It accepts
  `<a>@b.cz` and `a@b.cz,c.cz`, and it does not cap the local part at 64
  characters. Brevo will reject these downstream, so this is not a security
  issue.

  ### Assessment

  **Task quality:** Approved

  **Reasoning:** The implementation matches the brief and the controller
  rulings, the tests check real behaviour, and I found no correctness or
  security defect. The remaining items are small deviations and untested edge
  cases. The commit trailer needs the controller's check.

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ❌ Issues found: one verbatim-copy deviation in the footer (email.ts:273)
  and one unapproved addition to the ETF line (email.ts:220). Neither is in the
  test assertions, so the tests would not catch them.
  - Every other section, table and `{…}` placeholder in `content/email.md` (Top,
  1–5, Footer) is reproduced. The interfaces and rulings §15 #2, #6, #7, #8 and
  Ruling B are met.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff: the `/plan` route and the privacy page. The
  e-mail links to `/plan?…` and `/ochrana-osobnich-udaju/`, and neither is built
  yet (Task 8 and later). The `[Odhlásit se]` link is deliberately left as a
  visible placeholder.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff: the Task 7 TDD claim (RED then GREEN,
  36/36). I did not re-run the suite.

  ### Strengths
  - A single `Block[]` list feeds both the HTML and the plain-text part, so the
  two cannot drift apart.
  - Escaping happens once, in `inline()` (email.ts:154), plus `esc` on table
  heads, the CTA href and `<title>`. UTM values are never rendered, and the plan
  link is built from `toParams(sel)`, which carries only allow-listed values.
  - The test `online plan link carries only allow-listed params`
  (email.test.ts:117) injects `evil=<script>` and asserts it is absent.
  - Fee copy is computed, not typed. I checked the code against `data/`:
    - Fio tier 1 400 EUR / 3,95 EUR comes from `brokers.json` tiers[0].
    - The bank entry range (3–5 %) and ongoing range (1,75–2,59 %) come from
  `bank-funds.json` (ČSOB 3 / 2,59, KB 5 / 1,75, KID dates 2026-06-01 and
  2026-04-24).
    - ČS 1,65 comes from `inducements`.
    - Fio as_of 2026-07-01 matches the "od 1. 7. 2026" in email.md.
  - The plan model (plan.ts) has no ticker strings in logic. The only
  data-specific constant is `NEAREST` (plan.ts:433), keyed by index_key and
  commented.
  - Tie-break follows brief and §15 #2: VUAA comes first in etfs.json order and
  is the max-TER pick. This is the right call over the dispatch's "displayed
  order" wording.
  - plan.test.ts expected values agree with etfs.json. I checked the twin counts
  and distributions: sp500 gives acc, dist, acc, dist, acc, dist; nasdaq100
  gives acc, dist, acc; ftse_gac similar gives acc, dist. The tests also cover
  the VTI nearest, SCHD none and VT similar cases.
  - Check run: with the current data no pick ends up with an empty twin list
  after the `d=acc`/`d=dist` filter, so the empty-table risk in plan.ts:450 is
  latent only.

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  - **email.ts:273, footer is not verbatim.**
    - email.md has "protože jste si **ho** {11. 10. 2026} vyžádali na …". The
  code emits "jste si ${date} vyžádali", dropping "ho". Ruling: this approved
  copy must appear verbatim.
    - The test at email.test.ts:108 asserts '11. 10. 2026' and 'vyžádali na
  example.cz' separately, so it cannot catch this.
    - Fix: restore "ho" and assert the full sentence.
  - **email.ts:220, unapproved copy inserted into approved text.**
    - "(nejvyšší z vašich dvojčat: …)" is not in email.md. Either add it to
  email.md (a copy change Matěj must approve) or remove it.
    - Also, when there are no twins (SCHD only), `terTicker` falls back to the
  US pick's ticker (plan.ts:473), so the text would read "nejvyšší z vašich
  dvojčat: SCHD". That is wrong, because SCHD has no twin.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)
  - **email.ts:241**: the sentence "Pořadí v tabulce není doporučení." is an
  addition to email.md line 70. It is defensible, since the same file's line 51
  quotes "Pořadí není doporučení", but add it to email.md or drop it.
  - **email.test.ts**: the fee paragraphs have no assertions for Fio "3,95 EUR …
  3,16 %", ČS "1,65 %", the bank ranges "3–5 %" and "1,75–2,59 %", or the KID
  dates. These are the computed numbers most at risk, and a one-line assert each
  would cover them.
  - **email.ts:205**: `eg = 125` is a hand-typed number in `src/`. It is the
  approved example amount and is commented, but it needs an entry in decisions
  §15 as a product constant.
  - **plan.ts:450–451**: `kind` is not recomputed after the `dividends` filter.
  The current data has no case where it matters (see the check above). If a
  future fund set yields zero twins, the e-mail renders an empty table under
  "evropská ETF se stejným indexem".
  - **email.ts:313**: `new URL(siteUrl).host` throws on a malformed `SITE_URL`.
  That means a 500 in the function, so Task 8 should validate it at startup or
  fall back.

  ### Assessment

  **Task quality:** Needs fixes

  **Reasoning:** The architecture, escaping and data-driven numbers are solid,
  and the plan tests match etfs.json. Two copy deviations remain against the
  verbatim-copy ruling (the dropped "ho" in the footer, and the unapproved
  "nejvyšší z vašich dvojčat" insert, which is also wrong for the no-twin case).
  The tests do not pin the computed fee numbers.

  ⎿  Done (7 tool uses · 73.5k tokens · 56s)

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ✅ Spec compliant. All four listed files are present (api/lead.ts,
  api/lead.test.ts, src/lib/brevo.ts, scripts/dev.mjs). I checked §6a rules 1–4
  and 6 against docs/decisions.md:
    - Allow-list via `parseSelection` and `sanitizeUtm`: lead.ts:183-184.
    - Server-side e-mail validation: lead.ts:179.
    - 24 h dedupe through PLAN_SENT_AT: lead.ts:207-212.
    - An existing contact returns 2xx: lead.ts:212.
    - Honeypot gives a fake success with zero Brevo calls and one log line:
  lead.ts:177.
    - Rule 5 (rate limit) is correctly left out of the code, per the ruling.
    - §15 #11 order (upsert, send, PLAN_SENT_AT, with a failure of the last step
  only logged): lead.ts:211-224.
    - §15 #12 consent keys only when ticked, never cleared: lead.ts:186, 202.
    - §15 #13 JSON or 303 by `accept`: lead.ts:159-162.
    - 413 checked on byte length before parsing: lead.ts:165. Malformed or
  non-object JSON returns 400: lead.ts:170-171.
    - Non-string JSON values are ignored: lead.ts:172.
    - SITE_URL is validated with a fallback: lead.ts:232-233.
    - Brevo REST shapes, 8 s timeout, 404→null and status-only error messages:
  brevo.ts:302-331.
    - Dry-run logs only attribute keys and writes .dev/last-email.html:
  brevo.ts:287-298.
    - Env names match .env.example (checked). `.dev/` is in .gitignore
  (checked). astro.config.mjs:6 proxies `/api` to :8787 (checked).
  - Deviation (acceptable): `handleLead` takes an extra third argument `env`
  (lead.ts:156), which the brief's signature doesn't have. It is used only for
  the list IDs and is disclosed in the report.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff:
    - Brevo attribute types must exist in the account and match what we send.
  The controller should check this when the account is created in Phase 5:
      - NEWSLETTER: boolean
      - MONTHLY, YEARS, RETURN_PCT, CONSENT_TEXT_V: number
      - LAST_REQUEST_AT, NEWSLETTER_CONSENT_AT, PLAN_SENT_AT: we send full ISO
  timestamps (lead.ts:185). If these are created as Brevo *date* attributes,
  verify Brevo accepts a timestamp and not only `YYYY-MM-DD`. Otherwise create
  them as text. Dedupe parses PLAN_SENT_AT back with `Date.parse` (lead.ts:208),
  so a date-only value would weaken the 24 h window to day precision.
    - Vercel resolving the `.ts` imports is a known Phase 5 risk, not checked
  here.
    - The manual curl outputs exist only as a summary in task-8-report.md. The
  raw outputs the ruling asked for are not in the report.

  ### Strengths

  - The flow is tight and easy to read. Every failure path goes through the
  single `fail` and `ok` pair, so the JSON and no-JS behaviours can't drift
  apart.
  - The privacy rule is enforced at the source. The error message carries only
  the path segment and the status (brevo.ts:308), and a test proves the logs
  never contain the address (lead.test.ts:113-118).
  - The tests go beyond the brief's 11. They also cover:
    - getContact failure → 502
    - a failed PLAN_SENT_AT update still returns 200, with the exact log code
    - a server error on a form POST → 303 to /chyba-formulare/
    - malformed JSON → 400
    - the hostile hidden-fields test asserts the clamped and dropped values
  - An unticked newsletter box omits the keys rather than writing false, which
  matches GDPR art. 7(1).

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  - **lead.ts:216-218: the error's cause is thrown away.** `catch {
  log('brevo_error') }` drops the error message. In production every 502 looks
  the same, whether the cause is a 401 bad key, a 400 for a missing sender or
  unknown attribute, a timeout, or a `renderEmail`/`buildPlan` exception. That
  last case gets mislabelled as a Brevo error. This matters most on launch day,
  when a misconfigured env (e.g. empty `BREVO_SENDER_EMAIL`, which makes every
  send fail) can only be diagnosed from logs. brevo.ts:308 already keeps the
  address out of the message by design. Fix: `catch (err) { log('brevo_error', {
  error: String((err as Error)?.message ?? err).slice(0, 200) }); ... }` and
  the same at line 222. Keep the "no e-mail in logs" test green. The message
  from brevo.ts holds no address, and errors from render/plan don't include the
  input e-mail.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)
  - **lead.ts:204 and lead.ts:154: list handling is untested.** No test passes
  `env` to check that the plan list is always included and the newsletter list
  only when ticked, or that a missing or NaN ID is omitted. `listId` is new
  parsing logic. Add one test: `handleLead(json({email, newsletter:'on'}),
  deps(f), {BREVO_PLAN_LIST_ID:'3', BREVO_NEWSLETTER_LIST_ID:'x'})` → `lists`
  deep-equals `[3]`, plus the ticked/unticked pair.
  - **lead.ts:164: no early size check.** `req.text()` buffers the whole body
  before the 10 kB check. The ruling allows this, and Vercel caps request bodies
  anyway, but checking `content-length` first is a cheap early reject.
  - **lead.ts:207-215: double submits can race.** Two submits within
  milliseconds both see no PLAN_SENT_AT and both send. This is inherent to
  read-then-write on Brevo and acceptable. It deserves a one-line `shortcut:`
  comment.
  - **scripts/dev.mjs:264-265: orphaned dev server on Windows.** With `shell:
  true`, `astro.kill()` kills the `cmd` shell, not the npx/astro grandchild, so
  `astro dev` can keep holding :4321 after Ctrl+C. It is dev-only. A fix is
  `spawn('npx', ['astro','dev'], {shell:true, stdio:'inherit'})` plus `taskkill
  /pid <pid> /T /F` on win32, or just note it.
  - **scripts/dev.mjs:262 vs astro.config.mjs:6: address mismatch.** The server
  binds `127.0.0.1`, but the proxy targets `localhost`. That works today, since
  the report says the proxy test passed, but it depends on Node's dual-stack
  fallback. Bind `localhost` or target `127.0.0.1` so they match.

  `{error}`, and api/lead.ts:104 logs `plan_sent_at_update_failed` with

  METHOD <first path segment> failed: <status>`, so it has no e-mail address in
  it (the path segment is `contacts`, not the address). The new test at
  api/lead.test.ts:28-35 asserts the cause is logged and 'secret' is not. The
  rename to `lead_failed` is accurate, since render and plan errors land in the
  same catch.
  2. **List-ID test** — ADDRESSED. api/lead.test.ts:36-45 covers all three
  cases: `[3]`, `[3,4]`, `[3]`.
  3. **Dedupe shortcut comment** — ADDRESSED. The comment is at api/lead.ts:83,
  directly above `getContact`.
  4. **scripts/dev.mjs host binding and process-tree kill** — ADDRESSED. Server
  binds `localhost` (dev.mjs:143) and the base URL uses `localhost`
  (dev.mjs:128). On win32, `stop` runs `spawnSync('taskkill', ['/pid', pid,
  '/T', '/F'])` (dev.mjs:148). The report shows a POST through :4321 returning
  ok and nothing listening on 4321 or 8787 afterwards. The report admits real
  Ctrl+C was not tested; only the process.exit path was.

  Check: I did not re-run any tests; the report's counts (14/14 and 52/52) are
  consistent with the +2 tests in the diff.

  cosmetic and slightly misleading. If `localhost` resolves to ::1 first, it
  could differ from that message, but the Astro proxy uses the same name, so
  that is consistent.

  None.

  **Fix round:** All findings addressed, no new Critical/Important breakage.

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ✅ Spec compliant for everything verifiable from the diff. All 10 components
  and 4 pages from the brief exist. Section order and `data-section` hooks are
  correct (src/pages/index.astro:591-597). Copy matches content/page.md §1–§9
  word for word: I compared every section by hand, and the `*Poznámka:*` lines
  are left out. Every `{…}` is computed at build time.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff:
    - The first screen at 390×844 has not been checked visually. The implementer
  only did arithmetic and had no browser. The controller or Phase 5 must take a
  real screenshot of variants a and b.
    - The decisions §10 "ticker pair" look depends on Task 10's twin rows.
    - Whether Task 10 unhides `[data-result]` / `[data-sticky]` and fills
  `data-broker-note` for the default "Nevím" state. page.md §2 has copy for that
  note ("počítáme s nejvyšší zveřejněnou přirážkou za směnu, {0,5 %}"), and the
  static skeleton leaves it empty, which is what ruling E asks for.

  ### Strengths
  - One source for the data rules. NoJsTable
  (src/components/NoJsTable.astro:231) and the default result (Tool.astro:316)
  both go through `buildPlan`, so the twin order and the highest-TER rule are
  not reimplemented.
  - The hero B / Step B default numbers, KID dates, broker price-list dates, and
  the "Nevím" broker (highest `fx_pct`) are all derived (Tool.astro:316-320,
  377-396).
    - I checked them against data/bank-funds.json and data/brokers.json: XTB 0,5
  % with 29. 4. 2026; T212 has no date, so it shows "Trading 212"; the bank
  funds give 3–5 % and 1,75–2,59 %. All match page.md.
  - LeadForm (src/components/LeadForm.astro:185-218) matches the brief:
    - honeypot markup is exact;
    - the hidden `t m y r b d` and `utm_*` fields are there, with defaults from
  `RANGES[3]` (same tuple shape as the Tool.astro:324-326 destructuring);
    - status is `role=status aria-live=polite`;
    - the checkbox is unticked;
    - each form gets unique ids per `location`, so there are no duplicate ids on
  index.
  - The privacy page (src/pages/ochrana-osobnich-udaju.astro) covers every art.
  13 point in the ruling. It names the legitimate interest. The art. 21
  objection is in its own block, which art. 21(4) requires. Portability is
  correctly limited to processing based on (b) and (a). Retention text matches
  data/sources.md §9.2 point 5 (checked lines 318-320) and is marked `[DOPLNIT:
  potvrdit lhůty]`.
  - Accessibility basics are in place: one `<h1>` holding two spans
  (Hero.astro:156-159), labels or a legend on every input, tap targets ≥ 44 px
  (global.css:747, 757, 760, 767, 779), `:focus-visible` (global.css:717), and
  the `[hidden]` override.
  - The test pins the NBSP and en-dash output of the new formatters
  (src/lib/data.test.ts:479-483).

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  1. **src/pages/chyba-formulare.astro:533 together with api/lead.ts:22: every
  failure on the no-JS form tells the visitor their e-mail is wrong.**
     - `api/lead.ts:22` redirects every non-OK reason (invalid e-mail, 502 Brevo
  failure, rate limit) to `/chyba-formulare/`. That page shows only the
  "Invalid" copy: "Tenhle e-mail nevypadá úplně správně."
     - Result: when Brevo is down or the rate limit triggers, a no-JS visitor
  with a valid address is told to fix it. The approved copy for "Server error"
  and "Rate limit" (page.md §2 States) is never shown.
     - The implementer flagged this as concern 2. I checked `api/lead.ts` (grep
  for the redirect) and `api/lead.test.ts:108` (it confirms `down` →
  `/chyba-formulare/`).
     - Fix: add a second static page, e.g. `/chyba-odeslani/`, with the
  Server-error copy and the form again. Redirect `invalid`/`empty` to
  `/chyba-formulare/` and everything else to the new page. That is about a
  one-line change in the API plus a ~15-line page.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)
  1. **TER shows "0,3 %" instead of "0,30 %".** This affects
  src/components/NoJsTable.astro:249, and the built dist/index.html has three
  "0,3 %" occurrences. page.md §9 shows "0,30 %". Fix with a TER-only formatter
  using `minimumFractionDigits: 2`, applied here, in the e-mail, and in Task 10.
  This needs a ruling (implementer concern 1).
  2. **Bank fee ranges are computed over different fund lists** (Tool.astro:390
  vs 391). The entry-fee range uses all of `BANKS`, including the ČS "category"
  row with `ongoing_pct: null`. The ongoing range and `compare` (fees.ts:48) use
  only `kidBanks`. Today both give 3–5 % by coincidence. If the ČS entry fee
  changes, the "Jak počítáme" text would quote a fee for a fund that isn't in
  the calculation. Use `kidBanks` for both.
  3. **Tool.astro:322-323, 384, 395: hard-coded broker label maps.**
  `brokerShort[b.id]` would print "undefined" for any broker added to
  brokers.json later. Today the ids are xtb, t212, fio and cs, all mapped. Add a
  `label` field to brokers.json or a fallback to `b.id`.
  4. **No `aria-live` on the result block.** `[data-result-body]`
  (Tool.astro:416) sits inside a `hidden` container. The global constraint asks
  for "aria-live on the result", and only `.result-line` (Tool.astro:375) has
  it. Task 10 should either add `aria-live` to the result body or move focus to
  the result heading.
  5. **Tool.astro:385: the "Jak počítáme" link doesn't open the section.**
  `href="#jak-pocitame"` points at a closed `<details>`, and following a
  fragment link does not open it. Task 10 should open it on click.
  6. **src/lib/data.ts:507: formatting slip.** It reads `fmtDate =(iso` (missing
  space). The new exports were also inserted between `fmtCzk` and `fmtDate` and
  split the formatter group.
  7. **ochrana-osobnich-udaju.astro:675 reuses the tool's `.result` class** for
  the objection box. This couples legal-page styling to the tool's result block.
  Use its own class, e.g. a shared `.box`.
  8. **PlanContents.astro:271: `/plan-preview.png` doesn't exist yet,** so the
  live page shows a broken-image frame until Task 13. This is planned; it just
  must not ship to an ad.

  ### Assessment

  **Task quality:** Needs fixes

  **Reasoning:** The markup, copy match, data derivation and legal page are
  solid and follow the rulings. The no-JS error path, though, gives server
  errors and rate limits the wrong message ("e-mail looks wrong"), so a small
  API change and one extra static page are needed. TER formatting needs a
  controller ruling.

  Not checked: I did not run the build or tests (I relied on the reported
  results and the existing dist/index.html), and did no visual or first-screen
  check.

● Agent(Review Task 10 (spec + quality))
  ⎿  Done (10 tool uses · 89.4k tokens · 1m 46s)
  (ctrl+o to expand)

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ✅ Spec compliant for everything I could check against the diff. The rulings
  are followed: Ruling C, because no `tool-data` JSON element was added and the
  island imports `plan.ts`, `fees.ts`, `selection.ts` and `data.ts` directly
  (src/scripts/tool.ts:242-248). Ruling E, because only hooks were added to
  Tool.astro: `data-expand`, `tabindex="-1"` on the step headings and
  `aria-live` on the result body (Tool.astro diff lines 64, 77, 120, 135).
  - Deferred item (a) is done: `aria-live="polite"` is on `[data-result-body]`
  (diff line 135). Deferred item (b) is done: one document-level handler opens
  `details#jak-pocitame` (tool.ts:410-412).
  - Hidden fields: `t m y r b d` are synced on every `.lead-form` from
  `toParams` (tool.ts:375-377). I checked LeadForm.astro:36-41 and it has all
  six fields, so the `namedItem(...)` cast cannot return null. `utm_*` is left
  alone.
  - `tool:result` is dispatched when there is at least one pick (tool.ts:379).
  "Jiné" never enters `t`, because `read()` filters `US_TICKERS` only
  (tool.ts:279).
  - Ad B: the variant is read from `dataset` (tool.ts:420). VOO is preselected,
  Step A collapses and "Vybráno: VOO · změnit" re-expands it (tool.ts:413-417).
  - Sliders: values update in rAF on `input` and live regions are turned off
  while dragging. On `change` they go back to `polite` and render again
  (tool.ts:391-404).
  - Animation: it is CSS and class-triggered. Only the group of the chip just
  switched on gets `.enter`, never on load or on filter or slider changes
  (tool.ts:313, 387, 426). The stagger is capped at 6 (tool.ts:291) and
  `prefers-reduced-motion` gives `animation: none` (global.css diff line 465).
  - Copy: I compared it line by line with content/page.md lines 88-201. Step A
  texts, the VTI, SCHD and Jiné texts, twin rows, the ticker note, "Pořadí není
  doporučení.", the broker notes, the fixed-fee note with the ČNB label, the
  result pair, same-index, fee and small-print lines, and the sticky line are
  all verbatim. The broker notes start with a capital letter ("Počítáme…",
  "Ceník…") because they are standalone sentences; page.md has them lowercase
  after a colon. That is acceptable.
  - No `innerHTML`. Every node is built with `h()`/`append(string)`
  (tool.ts:251-257).
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff alone. The controller should check these in a
  browser:
    - variant B puts Step B in the first screen at 390×844;
    - the strike and row timing looks right;
    - the collapsed Step A shows only the h2 and the button;
    - a screen reader announces the result on first reveal (see Minor 1);
    - the `data-section` attributes still exist on the sections (they are not in
  this diff).

  ### Strengths

  - Logic is reused, not re-implemented. Groups, twins, filtering and fees all
  come from `buildPlan` and `compare`. The fixed-fee EUR amount reuses
  `commissionEur` and `brokerFor` (tool.ts:360). Every number shown is computed
  from data, including `0,5 %` via `brokerFor('unknown')`, the ČNB date from
  `FX.validFor` and the 7 in "těchto 7" from `US_TICKERS.length`.
  - `FX_BROKER` and `brokerShort` were moved into data.ts, so the static
  skeleton and the island cannot drift apart (data.ts diff lines 202-205).
  - The reduced-motion handling is careful. The comment at global.css:464
  explains why the global rule is not enough: delays plus `both` fill would keep
  the rows invisible. The forced-colours fallback (global.css:466) is a good
  catch.
  - `plural()` is correct for Czech, where 12–14 take "let" (no mod-10 rule),
  and it is tested (data.test.ts diff lines 183-186).
  - A named risk checked: could a group end up with 0 twins under the acc/dist
  filter, which would render "→ (0 fondů…)"? I ran a scratchpad script over
  `buildPlan` for every ticker × acc/dist. With the current data no `same` or
  `nearest` group is ever empty. QQQ/dist gives 1 twin and VT gives 1 twin + 1
  similar.

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  None.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)

  1. **tool.ts:378, Tool.astro:135. The first result may not be announced.** The
  result body is a live region inside `[data-result][hidden]`. It is unhidden
  in the same task its content is inserted, and many screen readers do not
  announce content that appears together with its container. The Step A
  `[data-twins]` live region partly covers this. If the controller's
  screen-reader check confirms the problem, the fix is to move focus to the
  result heading on the first reveal, which the dispatch allowed as an
  alternative.
  2. **tool.ts:258, 392-403. No final announcement after a pointer drag.**
  During a drag, the rAF renders already write the final text while
  `aria-live="off"`. On `change`, `setText` skips the unchanged nodes, so
  `.result-line` (which uses only `setText`) gets no mutation and says nothing.
  Keyboard and screen-reader swipe adjustments are fine, because `input` and
  `change` fire together and the rAF is cancelled. Whether this needs fixing is
  the controller's call.
  3. **tool.ts:301 and 327. Hand-typed fallback `?? 'S&P 500'`.** This index
  name is typed by hand, which the "no number typed by hand" rule forbids in
  spirit. With the current data it is unreachable (VTI always has 3 twins), so
  the plain fallback `g.indexName` or dropping the `??` would do.
  4. **tool.ts:346-349. The TER source fields are looked up again.** They come
  from `UCITS.find(...).sources.default.as_of` instead of the plan's `Twin`
  (`asOf`, `issuer`), which already holds them, so the same field is resolved
  twice. If `as_of` is null, the line also renders a dangling "stav k ".
  5. **data.ts:203 vs fees.ts:17-21. "Highest FX surcharge" is computed in two
  places.** `FX_BROKER` (`reduce`) and `brokerFor('unknown')` (`Math.max`) give
  the same value today. This logic predates the task (it moved from Tool.astro),
  so it is noted only.
  6. **tool.ts:379. `tool:result` fires on every rAF frame.** The report flags
  this. Task 11 must treat the event as idempotent and must not attach anything
  per event.

  ### Assessment

  **Task quality:** Approved

  **Reasoning:** Every requirement in the brief and the controller rulings that
  can be checked in code is met. The copy is verbatim from page.md, all numbers
  come from data through the shared lib functions, and there is no innerHTML.
  The remaining points are minor accessibility and live-region refinements that
  need the controller's browser and screen-reader check to confirm.

  I did not run a browser or screen reader and did not type-check. The only
  command I ran was the scratchpad edge-case script over `buildPlan`.

● Agent(Review Task 11 (spec + quality)) Sonnet 5.5
  ⎿  Done (7 tool uses · 67.7k tokens · 43s)
  (ctrl+o to expand)

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ❌ Issues found: one requirement is broken on the "Zadat znovu" path
  (Important #1 below). Everything else I could check against the diff matches
  the brief.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff:
    - Thank-you copy and table columns against page.md §8. I did not diff them
  against the file.
    - All DOM, focus, IntersectionObserver, share/clipboard and 429/timeout
  behaviour. The implementer had no browser, and neither did I.
    - That `Tool.astro` still renders `#lead-inline` and `#lead-footer` on every
  page that loads this script (checked: only `Tool.astro` and `FinalForm.astro`
  use `LeadForm`). If the script ever lands on a page without `[data-result]`,
  `[data-sticky]` or `#lead-footer`, the top-level `q()` calls return null and
  the whole script throws.
    - The inline form's hidden fields are in sync with the tool state at submit
  time. I checked this only through the report.

  ### Strengths
  - It reuses `validateEmail`, `parseSelection`, `buildPlan` and `sanitizeUtm`.
  The thank-you table is built from the submitted form's own fields, as the
  controller required.
  - It uses no `innerHTML`; the address goes in through `append(string)`.
  - The `tool:result` handler adds one listener per page and writes only when
  the text differs (`setText`, forms.ts:101). That closes the Task 10 deferred
  minor. The catch-up for ad B (forms.ts:109) covers the script-ordering gap.
  - Error handling is complete:
    - `AbortSignal.timeout(10000)` and a `res.json().catch` fallback.
    - `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby` are set and cleared on the next
  input.
    - 429, 400 and other statuses map to the right copy.
    - The button is re-enabled on every failure path.
    - The honeypot goes through in the JSON body.
  - UTMs are filled from `location.search` through the existing sanitizer.
  `namedItem(k)` is safe because `sanitizeUtm` returns exactly the five fields
  the form has.
  - The sticky bar logic is small, with the reduced-motion check and no modal.
  Phase 4 hook points are commented.

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  1. **forms.ts:140 vs 147-148: after a successful submit, "Zadat znovu" brings
  back a dead form.**
     - `return thankYou(...)` skips the button reset. The restored form
  (`f.hidden = false`, line 175) therefore has a disabled button reading
  "Posíláme…". Its status line also still says "Posíláme…".
     - The visitor who mistyped the address, which is the exact case this link
  exists for, cannot resubmit.
     - Fix: restore the button and clear the status either before calling
  `thankYou`, or inside the "Zadat znovu" handler. For example, set
  `btn.disabled = false`, `btn.textContent = label` and `status(f).textContent =
  ''` there.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)
  - forms.ts:155-158: the row-building one-liner is dense (a nested `flatMap`
  with tuple casts). It is hard to read and has no tests. Splitting it into a
  small `rowsFor(group)` helper would help.
  - forms.ts:104: the footer heading and body switch never revert if all picks
  are removed. The sticky bar does handle that case, and the footer would keep
  saying "Pošleme vám plán pro …" with stale tickers. This is cosmetic.
  - forms.ts:99-100: `footH.nextElementSibling` as the body paragraph is a
  fragile coupling to `LeadForm.astro` markup. A `data-` attribute would be
  sturdier.
  - forms.ts:196-200: the `input` listener clears the status while "Posíláme…"
  is shown. Typing during a send blanks the live region. The effect is harmless.
  - forms.ts:163-164: `shareNote` is created as `role=status` and then filled
  later, as a freshly inserted live region. Some screen readers will not
  announce "Odkaz zkopírován". Giving it an initial non-empty state or inserting
  it empty beforehand would make it dependable.
  - The brief's Step 4 worklog line, saying dedupe was not verified in dry-run,
  is still missing. The report acknowledges this; the controller needs to add
  it.
  - `.thanks` has no CSS and the thank-you box is unstyled apart from reused
  classes. Expect a visual pass in Phase 5.
  - The report's gzip total of about 13.7 kB is under budget. I did not
  re-measure it.

  ### Assessment

  **Task quality:** Needs fixes

  **Reasoning:** The form flow, error mapping, a11y attributes and reuse of
  tested logic are solid, and the listener is cheap. The one blocking defect is
  the disabled "Posíláme…" button that comes back with the restored form after
  "Zadat znovu", which is a one-line fix.
● One Important finding: after "Zadat znovu", the restored form's button stays
  disabled, which is exactly the case where the visitor needs to resubmit. Fix
  round 1 also covers three cheap minors.

  `src/scripts/forms.ts:104` passes a reset callback to `thankYou`. It sets
  `btn.disabled = false`, restores `label` and clears the status. `forms.ts:143`
  calls `reset()` before `f.hidden = false` and `emailOf(f).focus()`. The
  address is kept because the form stays in the DOM. The success path returns
  early, so the button stays disabled until reset, which is now handled.
  - **shareNote live region inserted empty** — ADDRESSED. `forms.ts:128-131`
  creates the span empty, with `role=status` and `aria-live=polite`, and inserts
  it with the thank-you box. Text is set only in the copy handler
  (`forms.ts:148`). One residual risk: `h('span', '')` is called with the third
  argument omitted. It is harmless if `h` handles an empty child list, and I did
  not check `h`.
  - **Explicit data- hooks, and footer revert on no picks** — ADDRESSED.
    - `LeadForm.astro:29-30` adds `data-lead-h` and `data-lead-body`.
  `forms.ts:48-49` queries them inside the `#lead-footer` form, which is the
  form element itself, so the scope is right.
    - `footDefault` is captured at load, before the `onResult` catch-up call.
  The values equal the `FinalForm.astro` props, which match page.md §7.
    - `syncFooter` restores them when `result.hidden` is true, and the existing
  MutationObserver on `[data-result]` now calls it (`forms.ts:57`). `syncFooter`
  is a hoisted declaration and runs only after load.
  - **Thank-you texts and table headers match page.md §8** — I did not
  independently re-diff against §8; I did not read §8. I only confirmed that the
  report states the comparison was done (report line "Compared … no
  deviation"). The fix diff made no text changes, and the six `th` strings are
  unchanged context. I did not verify the character-by-character claim.

  ### New Breakage in the Fix Diff

  None.

  ### Out-of-Scope Observations

  - The report says browser behaviour is still unverified: focus after "Zadat
  znovu", the aria-live announcement, and sticky and footer behaviour. These are
  worth a manual check in the Phase 5 QA.

  ### Verdict

  **Fix round:** All findings addressed, no new Critical/Important breakage. The
  caveat is the §8 text comparison (finding 4), which I took on the report's
  word and did not re-check. The report names `npx astro build` OK and `npm
  test` 56 pass, 0 fail. I did not re-run either.

› Message from general-purpose
  ### Spec Compliance

  - ✅ Spec compliant. Every item in the brief and the controller rulings that I
  could check against the diff is met.
    - **Files:** `src/pages/plan.astro` and `src/scripts/plan-page.ts` both
  exist.
    - **Parsing and reuse:** `plan-page.ts:213-215` uses `parseSelection(new
  URLSearchParams(location.search), US_TICKERS)` and `buildPlan(sel, FX)`. This
  is the allow-list path from decisions §6a rule 1, and there is no lookup.
    - **Same copy as the e-mail:** `plan-page.ts:215` renders `blocks(...)` from
  `email.ts`, so the page and the e-mail cannot drift. The only change to
  `email.ts` is `export` on `blocks` and `Block`, plus an optional `mail?: true`
  marker.
    - **E-mail-only blocks dropped:** `mail: true` sits on exactly five blocks:
  the greeting, the "Otevřít plán online" link, the "Plán vám přišel" footer,
  the art. 21(4) paragraph and the operator line. The operator line was not
  named in the brief. It is a defensible extra because the `Footer` component
  already carries it. The CTA is dropped twice over, by the marker and by `case
  'cta': return null` (`plan-page.ts:179`). The disclaimer, "Toto není
  doporučení" and the sources are kept.
    - **No innerHTML:** only `createElement`, `textContent` and `append(string)`
  are used. `a.href` comes from a regex limited to `https?:`, and the link text
  is set through `textContent`. I did not re-grep `dist`. I take the report's
  count of 0 as unverified.
    - **Layout:** `plan.astro:8-24` has `noindex`, the title "Váš ETF plán | ETF
  Dvojče", a header link back to `/`, `<Footer />`, and `data-section="plan"`.
    - **No-JS:** `plan.astro:12` carries the approved microcopy and the home
  link. The CSS rules `html.js .no-js` and `html:not(.js) .needs-js` are at
  `global.css:58-59`.
    - **Analytics:** none added.
  - ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff:
    - **Malicious-param check:** the report claims the `t=<img…>,VOO&m=9e9`
  input gives `['VOO']` and 30000. That depends on `parseSelection` in
  `selection.ts`, which is not in the diff.
    - **Test count:** the report says 56/56 tests pass. I did not run them.
    - **JS sizes:** the report gives 5.5 kB gzip for the plan chunk and 8.1 kB
  gzip for the shared chunk. I did not check these.
    - **Browser rendering:** nothing was tested in a browser, and the report
  says so.
    - **Commit trailer:** the brief wants "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5". I
  did not see the commit message.
    - **Worklog:** the report says `docs/ai-worklog.md` was untouched. The
  constraint says subagent prompts and review findings must be logged. The
  controller should confirm this is handled.

  ### Strengths
  - The block list is reused instead of duplicating e-mail copy, and the
  `email.ts` change is minimal. The `mail` marker is optional, so the existing
  e-mail tests are unaffected.
  - `inline()` and `links()` in `plan-page.ts:145-165` follow the same grammar
  as the e-mail's `inline()`, but they only create text nodes. Nothing in the
  content can be parsed as HTML.
  - Tables are wrapped in `.table-wrap` with `th scope="col"`. The `.facts` and
  `<ol>` list variants are mapped.
  - The no-JS and needs-JS switch reuses the existing CSS classes. No new CSS
  was added.

  ### Issues

  #### Critical (Must Fix)
  - None.

  #### Important (Should Fix)
  - None.

  #### Minor (Nice to Have)
  - `plan-page.ts:193-197`: the first table header is an empty string, so
  `el('th', '')` produces an empty `<th scope="col">`. That is a small
  screen-reader annoyance. Giving it a visually hidden label, or using `<td>`,
  would fix it. The first column of each row also stays a `<td>` rather than
  `<th scope="row">`.
  - `plan-page.ts:156-163`: external links get no `rel="noopener noreferrer"`.
  This is low risk because the link set comes from fixed copy.
  - `plan-page.ts:173`: `render` returns `Node | null`, but `hr` and `cta`
  aside, every case returns a node. The `null` branch exists only for `cta`,
  which the `mail` filter already removes. This is harmless duplication.
  - `plan-page.ts:215`: `new Date()` is passed only for blocks that are then
  filtered out. This is fine, but it is coupled to the `mail` filter.

  ### Assessment

  **Task quality:** Approved

  **Reasoning:** The page renders the e-mail's block model with text nodes only,
  takes its input through the allow-listed `parseSelection`, and adds no
  dependencies and no tracking. The one `email.ts` change is minimal. The only
  findings are minor accessibility and tidiness points.

  ⎿  Done (36 tool uses · 156.6k tokens · 3m 10s)

› Message from general-purpose
  ## Phase 3 final review (e5b81c4..5fc1352)

  I did this in one pass by myself, without subagents. I read the ledger, the
  plan's Global Constraints and Review Focus, decisions §6/§6a, page.md and
  email.md. I read in full: api/lead.ts, brevo.ts, selection.ts, fees.ts,
  plan.ts, data.ts, email.ts, tool.ts, forms.ts, plan-page.ts, all components,
  Base, index, plan, privacy, thank-you and error pages, fetch-fx.mjs and
  size.mjs.

  Checks I ran:
  - `npm test`: 56/56 pass.
  - A scratch script in %TEMP% (the repo was not changed; a stray copy was
  deleted at once, and `git status` shows only the existing `docs/ai-worklog.md`
  change). It computed the fee results for every broker at 500, 3 000 and 30
  000 Kč, checked every US ticker × dividend filter for empty twin groups
  (none), and rendered an e-mail for ČS.

  I did not run `npm run build`: its prebuild step rewrites data/fx.json.
  Lighthouse and browser checks are out of scope.

  ### Strengths
  - **Fee maths matches the oracle and the copy.** With default inputs,
  `compare()` gives bank 272 000–339 000 Kč and ETF 22 000 Kč, the same as
  page.md §1B/§2. The Fio and ČS fixed-fee note turns on correctly: 3,21 % and
  3,25 % at 3 000 Kč, off at 30 000 Kč. `commissionEur` handles the tiers
  correctly, and the `pctOnly` guard (fees.ts:52) stops a pure-percentage fee
  from being called "pevný".
  - **The /api/lead trust boundary follows §6a rules 1–4 and 6.**
    - Rule 1: only `parseSelection` output and `sanitizeUtm` values reach Brevo.
  UTMs never reach the e-mail.
    - Dedupe reads `PLAN_SENT_AT` from Brevo and writes it only after a
  successful send (step 3 failure is logged, still 200). A duplicate returns 200
  with no send.
    - Honeypot gives a fake success and is logged. Logs never contain the
  address (tested). Size cap, 405, bad JSON and the 303 no-JS paths are all
  tested.
  - **Escaping is done once and in the right order.** `esc()` runs before the
  `**`/link markup (email.ts:19-20), and link URLs come only from data/etfs.json
  and siteUrl. /plan renders the same Block list with `textContent`/`append`
  only, never innerHTML. The island and forms.ts build everything with
  `createElement`.
  - **One plan model (`buildPlan`) feeds the island, the thank-you table, the
  e-mail, /plan and the no-JS table.** Ordering, grouping, the fallback plan and
  the "never a US TER" rule (plan.ts:59-62) are consistent everywhere.
  - **The hand-off between files works.** tool.ts `update()` writes `toParams`
  into every `.lead-form` hidden field. forms.ts sends `FormData` as JSON, and
  the server parses the same names, including the `fld_q8` honeypot and
  `newsletter=on`. FinalForm switches between the fallback and the visitor's
  picks.
  - **Copy fidelity is high.** Hero, steps A–C, result, states, Why,
  PlanContents, About, FAQ, Footer and NoJsTable match page.md word for word,
  apart from the deviations the ledger already rules on.

  ### Issues

  #### Critical
  None.

  #### Important
  1. **The e-mail has no working unsubscribe link: it shows the literal text
  "[Odhlásit se]".**
     - Where: src/lib/email.ts:138.
     - What: `LINK` only matches `[label](url)`, so `[Odhlásit se]` goes out as
  plain bracket text in both the HTML and the text part. Nothing else in src/api
  adds an unsubscribe link or a `List-Unsubscribe` header, and Brevo's
  transactional `/smtp/email` does not add one by itself.
     - Why it matters: the form, the FAQ and decisions §2 all promise "Odhlásíte
  se jedním klikem". As shipped, that promise is false, and a reviewer opening
  the real e-mail will see the brackets.
     - Fix: decide the mechanism now. Options are a Brevo unsubscribe tag or
  link (verify it in Phase 5), a `mailto:` reply-to link, or sending `headers: {
  'List-Unsubscribe': …, 'List-Unsubscribe-Post': 'List-Unsubscribe=One-Click'
  }` in `sendEmail`. If it stays a placeholder, render it as a marked `[DOPLNIT:
  odkaz pro odhlášení]` so it ends up on the README must-fill list. The Task 13
  open-items list only covers the header, not the visible body link.
  2. **Broker names are in the wrong grammatical case in the e-mail.**
     - Where: email.ts:73 and :76.
     - What: the ČS e-mail reads "nakupujete u Česká spořitelna" and "směna
  měny: u Česká spořitelna (ceník k 1. 2. 2026)". Fio gives "u Fio banka
  (e-Broker)". It should be "u České spořitelny" / "u Fio".
     - Why it matters: Fio/ČS visitors get this in every e-mail, and the
  Czech-language quality is part of the review.
     - Fix: a small genitive map (the same idea as `SHORT` in data.ts:
  `{xtb:'XTB', t212:'Trading 212', fio:'Fio', cs:'České spořitelny'}`). You can
  also fix the deferred "směna měny: u" colon at the same time, to match
  email.md's "směna měny u {brokera}".

  #### Minor
  3. **forms.ts throws on the error pages, and the ledger is wrong about this.**
     - Where: forms.ts:24-39, chyba-odeslani.astro:14 and
  chyba-formulare.astro:14.
     - What: both error pages render FinalForm, which imports forms.ts. The
  ledger says "only index uses it". On those pages `#lead-inline`,
  `[data-result]` and `[data-sticky]` are null, so
  `IntersectionObserver.observe(null)` throws before the submit handlers attach.
     - Effect: a JS visitor there gets a plain-POST form (it still works) plus a
  console error. Analytics hooks (Phase 4) will also be missing there.
     - Fix: guard the inline/sticky part with `if (inline && result && sticky)`.
  4. **"Poslat plán" in "Co vám přijde e-mailem" always goes to the top of the
  tool.**
     - Where: PlanContents.astro:23.
     - What: it always jumps to `#nastroj`. page.md §4 says it should scroll to
  the inline form when the result is shown. No script handles `data-cta="plan"`.
  5. **Server and browser disagree about ad variant B.**
     - Where: the Base.astro head script requires the `ad_b_` prefix;
  `adVariant` (selection.ts:77) also accepts a bare `ad_b`.
     - Effect: `utm_content=ad_b` shows hero A but is stored as `AD_VARIANT=b`.
  That skews attribution per ad. This is related to the deferred Task 6 minor,
  but this side effect wasn't recorded.
     - Fix: make both use one rule.
  6. **No-JS leads carry no UTMs.** The hidden UTM fields are only filled by
  forms.ts. No-JS traffic is tiny, but the README should say so.
  7. **The worst-case request takes much longer than the browser waits.** Four
  Brevo calls run one after another with 8 s timeouts each (brevo.ts:33), while
  the client gives up at 10 s (forms.ts:93). A slow Brevo can show "nepodařilo"
  while the e-mail is still sent. A retry is then caught by dedupe only if
  `PLAN_SENT_AT` was written. Check the Vercel Hobby function duration in Phase
  5.
  8. **A trailing slash in `SITE_URL` breaks links.** It produces
  `https://x.cz//plan?…` (verified) and `//ochrana-osobnich-udaju/`. Fix: strip
  it once in `POST()`, or document "no trailing slash" in .env.example.
  9. **Dry-run on Vercel returns 502 on every submit.** If `BREVO_API_KEY` is
  missing in a Vercel preview, dry-run writes to `.dev/` on a read-only
  filesystem, so every submit fails with 502. Fix: document it, or skip the file
  write when `process.env.VERCEL` is set.
  10. **The twin list is noisy for screen readers.** The `[data-twins]` live
  region (Tool.astro) re-renders the whole twin list on every chip tap, so
  screen readers re-read everything. Consider `aria-live` only on the short
  `.pair-line` summary.
  11. **`/plan-preview.png` is still missing**, so section 4 shows a broken
  image (Task 13 step, still open).

  ### Ledger triage (deferred minors)
  - **Fix before the checkpoint:**
    - Important #1 and #2 above.
    - The Task 7 "směna měny: u" colon (fold it into #2).
    - Task 11 "script throws on a page without hooks": it is reachable (#3).
    - Task 6 bare `ad_a`/`ad_b`: it now has an attribution effect (#5).
  - **Fine to defer to Phase 4/5:**
    - Font cache headers; FX source_url/atomic write; bank-range assert;
  `brokers.find!` (input is already validated by `parseSelection`); UTM trim
  order; EMAIL_RE leniency (Brevo validates again).
    - Content-length early check; Brevo attribute types (⚠ the dedupe depends on
  `PLAN_SENT_AT` keeping its time part: if it is created as a Brevo *date*
  attribute it may come back as date-only, so make it text or verify).
    - Live-region first reveal; empty `<th>` on /plan; `rel=noopener`; the
  share-line ticker personalisation (the ruling is fine).
    - "Kind not recomputed after filter": unreachable with current data. I
  checked every ticker × acc/dist/both and no group ends up empty.

  ### Declined to judge (set aside)
  - Rate limit (§6a rule 5): proposal only by plan §15 #17; Phase 5.
  - Vercel resolving `.ts`-extension imports and JSON import attributes in
  `api/`: already on the Task 13 open list; can't verify without deploy.
  - Analytics events: Phase 4.
  - Hero fit at 390×700, LCP/CLS, JS budget numbers: Task 13 / Phase 5, needs a
  browser/build.
  - Using the highest TER across all picks (VOO+QQQ → 0,30 % for the whole fee
  line): decisions §15 #2 makes this choice on purpose.
  - The thank-you table has no fee numbers, although decisions §6 says "fee
  numbers": page.md §8 (approved copy) wins.
  - Cross-site plain-form POST with a stranger's address: limited by the 24 h
  dedupe; the per-IP rate limit is Phase 5.
  - Legal accuracy of the privacy notice: flagged in the file itself as "lawyer
  confirms".

  ### Assessment
  **Ready to merge: With fixes.** The calculator, the plan model, escaping and
  the §6a boundary are correct and well tested. Before the Phase 3 checkpoint,
  two e-mail defects need fixing: the "[Odhlásit se]" placeholder with no link,
  and the wrong broker-name case.

  I did not check the Vercel runtime behaviour, the built dist output or real
  rendering. My numbers come from the source modules run directly in Node.

  3. **forms.ts throws on /chyba-formulare/ and /chyba-odeslani/** — ADDRESSED.

  the sticky click handler, `syncFooter` and the `tool:result` wiring. Both
  error pages render `FinalForm`, so the footer form exists and the unguarded
  `footH`/`footP` lookups (`forms.ts:56-57`) still resolve. The `.lead-form`
  submit wiring was not touched by the diff, so it stays unconditional. Verified

  `goToInline()`, which scrolls to the inline form and focuses the e-mail field.
  Otherwise the plain `href` goes to `#nastroj`, which is also the no-JS
  fallback. `goToInline` is shared with the sticky bar.
  5. **One ad-variant rule** — ADDRESSED. `selection.ts:351` uses
  `/^ad_([ab])_/`, and tests assert bare `ad_a` and `ad_b` give `unknown`.
  `Base.astro:32` tests `indexOf('ad_b_') === 0`, so both rules now agree.
  6. **UTM sanitising order** — ADDRESSED. `selection.ts:339` strips control
  characters, then trims, then slices to 100. The 150-space test passes by
  reading the code.
  7. **`SITE_URL` trailing slash** — ADDRESSED. `api/lead.ts` adds the exported
  `normalizeSiteUrl`, which strips trailing slashes after URL validation and
  falls back to localhost. `POST()` uses it. Tests cover a trailing slash, no
  slash, an invalid value and undefined.
  8. **Dry-run on Vercel** — ADDRESSED. `brevo.ts` skips the `mkdir`/`writeFile`
  when `process.env.VERCEL` is set and still logs. `.env.example` has the
  dry-run note. It is untested, which fits an env-dependent check.

  ### New Breakage in the Fix Diff

  None. One Minor point: the old `invalid_site_url` log line was removed, so a
  misconfigured `SITE_URL` now falls back to localhost silently. Links in the
  e-mail would then point at localhost with no log to explain it.

  ### Out-of-Scope Observations

  None.

  ### Verdict

  **Fix round:** All findings addressed, no new Critical/Important breakage. The
  report names its tests, claims 61/61 passing plus a clean build, and I did
  not re-run them. Items 3 and 4 have no automated test, so they need a browser
  check in Phase 5.

  Adding Playwright would fix that, and it needs your OK (details at the end).

  Tests: npm test passes 61 of 61. node data/validate.mjs is OK. astro build
  builds 6 pages (home, /plan, privacy, thank-you, two error pages), and none of
  them contains {…}, undefined, NaN or innerHTML.

  Bundle size: first-load JS is 13,8 kB gzipped against the 60 kB budget, and
  /plan is 13,7 kB. Run node scripts/size.mjs after a build to re-measure.

  End-to-end check (I ran this myself): the dev server serves the page. A POST
  to /api/lead returns {"ok":true}, and the no-JS invalid case redirects to
  /chyba-formulare/. The rendered e-mail has the right subject, ISINs and fee
  table, and its 20-year row matches calc-examples. Default numbers on the page
  match too: 22 000 Kč for the ETF against 272 000–339 000 Kč for the bank
  funds.

  Screenshots: not done. There is no Chrome or Edge here, and the built-in
  browser isn't available in this session (it can't open a local server anyway).
  So nothing visual has been checked: layout, whether hero B fits at 390×844 /
  390×700, the animation, focus, screen readers, font CLS. The "Co vám přijde
  e-mailem" section has no e-mail screenshot yet either, so it shows a broken
  image.

  Decision for you: may I add playwright as a dev dependency? It also downloads
  Chromium, about 150 MB. With it I'd take the hero screenshots and the e-mail
  preview image, and check those visual points. If not, the visual check moves
  to your phone in Phase 5.

  GDPR research: the form doesn't change. The plan e-mail rests on GDPR art.
  6(1)(b), storing picks and UTMs on art. 6(1)(f), and the newsletter on prior
  consent under § 7(2) of zákon 480/2004 Sb. Because of the 6(1)(f) part, I
  added one approved-style line to the e-mail footer explaining the right to
  object (art. 21(4)). Sources and my reasoning are in data/sources.md §9.
  That's my reading, not legal advice, so a lawyer should confirm it.

  Decisions I made on your behalf (full list in the ledger, each also in
  docs/decisions.md §15 or the worklog):
  1. ČS fee: 0,35 % with the 4 EUR minimum on every buy. This is still the open
     point I flagged when you approved the plan.
  2. Fonts: body text uses normal-width Archivo; tickers use a 4 kB wide-only
     subset. Total preloaded is 72 kB instead of 176 kB.
  3. SCHD-only plans price the ETF side with the S&P 500 twins, because a US
     ETF's fee must never appear as the ETF option.
  4. Fund fees always show two decimals ("0,30 %"), as page.md writes them.
  5. New page /chyba-odeslani/ for no-JS server errors. Without it, a failed
     send would tell people their e-mail is wrong.
  6. Copy added outside page.md: a visible "Časté otázky" heading on the FAQ,
     titles for the secondary pages, and "Odkaz zkopírován" after copying the
     share link. The /plan no-JS message is new too.
  7. Unsubscribe link is a visible [DOPLNIT: odkaz pro odhlášení] placeholder
     until the Brevo account exists. The site must not launch with it.
  8. Twin rows keep the " · " separators from page.md, which outranks the style
     note in §10.
  9. Subagent commits name the model that actually wrote them in the co-author
     line.

  Left for Phase 4–5:
  - Phase 4: Umami events.
  - Brevo and Vercel accounts, including Brevo attribute types. PLAN_SENT_AT
    must keep its time, or the 24-hour duplicate check weakens.
  - Rate-limit choice (Vercel firewall rule or Upstash) and the daily FX rebuild
    (GitHub Actions calling a Vercel deploy hook).
  - Whether Vercel resolves the .ts imports in api/.
  - Timing: four Brevo calls with 8 s timeouts each can outlast the browser's 10
    s wait, so a visitor may see an error while the e-mail still goes out.
  - A bad SITE_URL now falls back to localhost without logging anything.
  - LCP and CLS on throttled 4G, a real-inbox test, a screen-reader pass, and
    the [DOPLNIT] list in the README.

  you close this session.

● Phase 3 is finished and the screenshots exist now. Both heroes fit the first
  screen, with one small exception: hero B's trust line falls just below the
  fold at 390×700. The browser check found that the first build broke hero B,
  and that's fixed.

  What the screenshots caught: the first Playwright run showed hero A fitting,
  but hero B's fee result sat about 1 110 px down, far below the fold. The build
  had skipped the compact "Výnos trhu 5 % ročně · předpoklad, ne předpověď ·
  změnit" line that page.md §1B specifies. Instead it showed the full return
  slider and the UBS note above the result. The sticky bar also appeared on load
  over the first screen, and its button broke as "e-/mailem". No unit test or
  code review had caught any of this.

  After the fix:
  - Hero B at 390×700: headline, sub, both sliders, the return line and the full
    fee result line (272 000–339 000 Kč vs 22 000 Kč) are visible. The trust
    line sits about 90 px below the fold; at 390×844 everything fits, trust line
    included.
  - Sub-line: not shortened, because the layout fix was enough.
  - Hero A: unchanged; headline, chips and trust line are in the first screen at
    both sizes.
  - Sticky bar: appears only after the first interaction with the tool.
  - Missing spaces: Astro was dropping a space before inline links in 7 places
    ("2026.Jak počítáme"); those are fixed.

  Final numbers (I re-ran all of these myself):
  - 61/61 tests pass and the build makes 6 pages.
  - First-load JS is 13,9 kB gzipped against the 60 kB budget.
  - Tabular figures work: "1111" and "0000" measure the same width.
  - No console errors, failed requests or horizontal scrolling on any page.

  Files:
  - hero-a-390x844.png (docs/screenshots/hero-a-390x844.png)
  - hero-a-390x700.png (docs/screenshots/hero-a-390x700.png)
  - hero-b-390x844.png (docs/screenshots/hero-b-390x844.png)
  - hero-b-390x700.png (docs/screenshots/hero-b-390x700.png)
  - plan-preview.png (public/plan-preview.png): the real e-mail for VOO, used in
    "Co vám přijde e-mailem".
  - node scripts/screenshots.mjs re-runs the whole check after any build.

  Two new decisions on your behalf:
  - Step B now shows the result before "Kde nakupujete?" in both variants. That
    order differs from page.md §2, but the result updates live when the broker
    changes. If you don't like it, it's a markup move.
  - The earlier list of 9 decisions still stands. The two to look at are the ČS
    4 EUR minimum, and the unsubscribe placeholder, which must be filled before
    launch.

  Still open:
